Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on nucleus structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 363. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 112 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_363(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 363. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^112(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the nucleus object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 223. The moduli space has dimension at most 289 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on nucleus structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 223. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 289. QED.
